# Term and named-concept finder

These are navigation matches in machine OCR, not universal definitions. A term can have different meanings across installations; verify its source image. Heading matches are listed first; body matches may use the word in another sense. OCR can miss or misread a term. For definitions as supplied, start with [terminology and glossaries](topics/terminology.md).
